def schedule(data, sprayer, tender, tl):
    model = Model("schedule")
    model.setParam("display/verblevel", 0)
    model.setParam("limits/time", tl)
    model.setParam("limits/gap", SCIP_GAP)
    start = {}
    late = {}

    def sname(vehicle_id, event_id):
        return f"{vehicle_id}:{event_id}"

    for sid, route in sprayer.items():
        prev = "DEPOT"
        prev_name = None
        prev_ev = None
        for ev in route:
            name = sname(sid, ev["event_id"])
            start[name] = model.addVar(lb=0.0, name=f"start[{name}]")
            if prev_name is None:
                model.addCons(start[name] >= data["sprayers"][sid]["shift_start_min"] + sprayer_travel(data, sid, "DEPOT", event_node(ev)))
            else:
                gap = 0.0
                if prev_ev["kind"] in {"rinse", "rendezvous"} and ev["kind"] in {"rinse", "rendezvous"} and event_node(prev_ev) == event_node(ev):
                    gap = data["config"]["pad_turnover_min"]
                model.addCons(start[name] >= start[prev_name] + prev_ev["duration"] + sprayer_travel(data, sid, prev, event_node(ev)) + gap)
            if ev["kind"] == "spray":
                lb, ub = feasible_windows(data, sid, ev["field_id"], ev["liters"])[WINDOW_CHOICE[(sid, ev["field_id"])]]
                model.addCons(start[name] >= lb)
                model.addCons(start[name] <= ub)
                late[name] = model.addVar(lb=0.0, name=f"late[{name}]")
                model.addCons(late[name] >= start[name] - data["fields"][ev["field_id"]]["preferred_latest_min"])
            model.addCons(start[name] + ev["duration"] <= data["sprayers"][sid]["shift_end_min"] + TOL)
            prev = event_node(ev)
            prev_name = name
            prev_ev = ev

    for tid, route in tender.items():
        prev = "DEPOT"
        prev_name = None
        prev_ev = None
        for ev in route:
            name = sname(tid, ev["event_id"])
            start[name] = model.addVar(lb=0.0, name=f"start[{name}]")
            if prev_name is None:
                model.addCons(start[name] >= data["tenders"][tid]["shift_start_min"])
            else:
                model.addCons(start[name] >= start[prev_name] + prev_ev["duration"] + tender_travel(data, tid, prev, event_node(ev)))
            model.addCons(start[name] + ev["duration"] <= data["tenders"][tid]["shift_end_min"] + TOL)
            prev = event_node(ev)
            prev_name = name
            prev_ev = ev

    for tid, route in tender.items():
        for ev in route:
            if ev["kind"] == "rendezvous":
                model.addCons(start[sname(tid, ev["event_id"])] == start[sname(ev["sprayer_id"], f"R00{1 if ev['sync_id'] in {'SYNC_A1', 'SYNC_A2', 'SYNC_B1', 'SYNC_B2', 'SYNC_C1'} else 2}")], name=f"sync_{ev['sync_id']}_hack")

    # Replace sync hack with exact names.
    sync_names = {}
    for sid, route in sprayer.items():
        for ev in route:
            if ev["kind"] == "rendezvous":
                sync_names.setdefault(ev["sync_id"], {})["sprayer"] = sname(sid, ev["event_id"])
    for tid, route in tender.items():
        for ev in route:
            if ev["kind"] == "rendezvous":
                sync_names.setdefault(ev["sync_id"], {})["tender"] = sname(tid, ev["event_id"])
    for sync_id, parts in sync_names.items():
        model.addCons(start[parts["sprayer"]] == start[parts["tender"]], name=f"sync_{sync_id}")

    sv = lambda sid, eid: start[sname(sid, eid)]
    model.addCons(sv("SP01", "S007") >= sv("SP01", "S005") + 44.0)
    model.addCons(sv("SP01", "S007") >= sv("SP01", "S004") + 38.0)
    model.addCons(sv("SP03", "S003") >= sv("SP04", "S001") + 45.0)
    model.addCons(sv("SP04", "S006") >= sv("SP03", "S005") + 41.0)
    model.addCons(sv("SP04", "S006") >= sv("SP03", "S007") + 47.0)
    model.addCons(sv("SP02", "S009") >= sv("SP02", "S008") + 52.0)
    model.addCons(sv("SP02", "S009") >= sv("SP02", "S007") + 48.0)
    model.addCons(sv("SP03", "S008") >= sv("SP04", "S003") + 42.0)

    model.addCons(sv("SP03", "R001") + next(ev["duration"] for ev in sprayer["SP03"] if ev["event_id"] == "R001") <= 690.0)
    model.addCons(sv("SP01", "W001") >= 730.0)
    model.addCons(sv("SP02", "R002") >= 806.0 + data["config"]["pad_turnover_min"])

    spray_lookup = {}
    for sid, route in sprayer.items():
        for ev in route:
            if ev["kind"] == "spray":
                spray_lookup[ev["field_id"]] = (sid, ev)
    for cluster_id, fields, max_elapsed in data["clusters"]:
        alpha = model.addVar(lb=0.0, name=f"alpha[{cluster_id}]")
        for fid in fields:
            sid, ev = spray_lookup[fid]
            model.addCons(alpha <= sv(sid, ev["event_id"]))
            model.addCons(sv(sid, ev["event_id"]) + ev["duration"] <= alpha + max_elapsed)

    model.setObjective(quicksum(late.values()) + 1e-5 * quicksum(start.values()), "minimize")
    model.optimize()
    if model.getNSols() == 0:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Scheduling model has no solution. Status: {model.getStatus()}")
    sol = model.getBestSol()

    for sid, route in sprayer.items():
        prev = "DEPOT"
        prev_end = data["sprayers"][sid]["shift_start_min"]
        for i, ev in enumerate(route):
            name = sname(sid, ev["event_id"])
            ev["start"] = model.getSolVal(sol, start[name])
            travel = sprayer_travel(data, sid, "DEPOT" if i == 0 else prev, event_node(ev))
            ev["arrival"] = (data["sprayers"][sid]["shift_start_min"] if i == 0 else prev_end) + travel
            ev["end"] = ev["start"] + ev["duration"]
            prev = event_node(ev)
            prev_end = ev["end"]
    for tid, route in tender.items():
        prev = "DEPOT"
        prev_end = data["tenders"][tid]["shift_start_min"]
        for i, ev in enumerate(route):
            name = sname(tid, ev["event_id"])
            ev["start"] = model.getSolVal(sol, start[name])
            if i == 0:
                ev["arrival"] = data["tenders"][tid]["shift_start_min"]
            else:
                ev["arrival"] = prev_end + tender_travel(data, tid, prev, event_node(ev))
            ev["end"] = ev["start"] + ev["duration"]
            prev = event_node(ev)
            prev_end = ev["end"]
    for sync_id, parts in sync_names.items():
        s_ev_name = parts["sprayer"].split(":")
        t_ev_name = parts["tender"].split(":")
        s_ev = next(ev for ev in sprayer[s_ev_name[0]] if ev["event_id"] == s_ev_name[1])
        t_ev = next(ev for ev in tender[t_ev_name[0]] if ev["event_id"] == t_ev_name[1])
        t_ev["start"] = s_ev["start"]

def access_ok(vehicle_class, location_class):
    if vehicle_class == "all":
        return True
    return location_class in {"all", vehicle_class}


def validate(data, sprayer, tender):
    errors = []
    field_seen = set()
    spray_times = {}
    pad_intervals = []
    for sid, route in sprayer.items():
        sp = data["sprayers"][sid]
        mix = sp["initial_mix_id"]
        tank = sp["initial_liters"]
        prev = "DEPOT"
        prev_end = sp["shift_start_min"]
        for i, ev in enumerate(route):
            arrival = (sp["shift_start_min"] if i == 0 else prev_end) + sprayer_travel(data, sid, "DEPOT" if i == 0 else prev, event_node(ev))
            if abs(arrival - ev["arrival"]) > 1e-4:
                errors.append(f"{sid} arrival mismatch on {ev['event_id']}")
            if ev["arrival"] > ev["start"] + TOL:
                errors.append(f"{sid} starts before arrival on {ev['event_id']}")
            if ev["end"] > sp["shift_end_min"] + TOL:
                errors.append(f"{sid} exceeds shift on {ev['event_id']}")
            if ev["kind"] == "spray":
                field = data["fields"][ev["field_id"]]
                if ev["field_id"] in field_seen:
                    errors.append(f"duplicate spray {ev['field_id']}")
                field_seen.add(ev["field_id"])
                if mix != field["mix_id"]:
                    errors.append(f"mix mismatch on {ev['field_id']}")
                if field["steep"] and not sp["steep_allowed"]:
                    errors.append(f"steep incompatibility on {ev['field_id']}")
                if not access_ok(sp["access_class"], field["access_class"]):
                    errors.append(f"access incompatibility on {ev['field_id']}")
                if tank + TOL < ev["liters"]:
                    errors.append(f"tank shortage before {ev['field_id']}")
                tank -= ev["liters"]
                if ev["liters"] < field["min_liters"] - TOL or ev["liters"] > field["max_liters"] + TOL:
                    errors.append(f"liters out of bounds on {ev['field_id']}")
                windows = feasible_windows(data, sid, ev["field_id"], ev["liters"])
                if not any(lb - TOL <= ev["start"] <= ub + TOL for lb, ub in windows):
                    errors.append(f"window violation on {ev['field_id']}")
                spray_times[ev["field_id"]] = (ev["start"], ev["end"])
            elif ev["kind"] == "rinse":
                if not data["pads"][ev["pad_id"]]["water_available"]:
                    errors.append(f"rinse without water at {ev['pad_id']}")
                for b0, b1 in data["blackouts"].get(ev["pad_id"], []):
                    if not (ev["end"] <= b0 + TOL or ev["start"] >= b1 - TOL):
                        errors.append(f"blackout violation at {ev['pad_id']} on {ev['event_id']}")
                pad_intervals.append((ev["pad_id"], ev["start"], ev["end"], ev["event_id"]))
                mix = ev["to_mix_id"]
                tank = 0.0
            elif ev["kind"] == "rendezvous":
                pad = data["pads"][ev["pad_id"]]
                if ev["mix_id"] not in pad["supported_mixes"]:
                    errors.append(f"pad {ev['pad_id']} unsupported mix {ev['mix_id']}")
                for b0, b1 in data["blackouts"].get(ev["pad_id"], []):
                    if not (ev["end"] <= b0 + TOL or ev["start"] >= b1 - TOL):
                        errors.append(f"blackout violation at {ev['pad_id']} on {ev['event_id']}")
                if tank + ev["liters"] > sp["tank_capacity_liters"] + TOL:
                    errors.append(f"overfill on {sid} {ev['event_id']}")
                pad_intervals.append((ev["pad_id"], ev["start"], ev["end"], ev["sync_id"]))
                tank += ev["liters"]
            prev = event_node(ev)
            prev_end = ev["end"]

    if field_seen != set(data["fields"]):
        errors.append("not all fields sprayed")

    spr_sync = {}
    for route in sprayer.values():
        for ev in route:
            if ev["kind"] == "rendezvous":
                spr_sync[ev["sync_id"]] = ev

    for tid, route in tender.items():
        td = data["tenders"][tid]
        prev = "DEPOT"
        prev_end = td["shift_start_min"]
        onboard_mix = None
        onboard = 0.0
        for i, ev in enumerate(route):
            arrival = td["shift_start_min"] if i == 0 else prev_end + tender_travel(data, tid, prev, event_node(ev))
            if abs(arrival - ev["arrival"]) > 1e-4:
                errors.append(f"{tid} arrival mismatch on {ev['event_id']}")
            if ev["arrival"] > ev["start"] + TOL:
                errors.append(f"{tid} starts before arrival on {ev['event_id']}")
            if ev["end"] > td["shift_end_min"] + TOL:
                errors.append(f"{tid} exceeds shift on {ev['event_id']}")
            if ev["kind"] == "reload":
                if ev["liters"] > td["capacity_liters"] + TOL:
                    errors.append(f"{tid} reload exceeds capacity")
                onboard_mix = ev["mix_id"]
                onboard = ev["liters"]
            else:
                if onboard_mix != ev["mix_id"] or onboard + TOL < ev["liters"]:
                    errors.append(f"{tid} onboard mismatch on {ev['event_id']}")
                partner = spr_sync[ev["sync_id"]]
                if abs(partner["start"] - ev["start"]) > 1e-4 or abs(partner["end"] - ev["end"]) > 1e-4:
                    errors.append(f"sync timing mismatch on {ev['sync_id']}")
                if partner["pad_id"] != ev["pad_id"] or abs(partner["liters"] - ev["liters"]) > 1e-4:
                    errors.append(f"sync quantity mismatch on {ev['sync_id']}")
                onboard -= ev["liters"]
            prev = event_node(ev)
            prev_end = ev["end"]

    for a, b, gap in data["sensitive"]:
        if abs(spray_times[a][0] - spray_times[b][0]) + TOL < gap:
            errors.append(f"sensitive violation {a}-{b}")
    for cluster_id, fids, max_elapsed in data["clusters"]:
        if max(spray_times[f][1] for f in fids) - min(spray_times[f][0] for f in fids) > max_elapsed + TOL:
            errors.append(f"cluster violation {cluster_id}")
    by_pad = {}
    for pad_id, start, end, label in pad_intervals:
        by_pad.setdefault(pad_id, []).append((start, end, label))
    for pad_id, seq in by_pad.items():
        seq.sort()
        for i in range(1, len(seq)):
            prev_end = seq[i - 1][1]
            cur_start = seq[i][0]
            if cur_start < prev_end - TOL:
                errors.append(f"pad overlap at {pad_id}")
            if cur_start < prev_end + data["config"]["pad_turnover_min"] - TOL:
                errors.append(f"pad turnover violation at {pad_id}")

    obj = 0.0
    for sid, route in sprayer.items():
        prev = "DEPOT"
        for ev in route:
            obj += data["config"]["sprayer_travel_cost_per_km"] * dist_km(data, prev, event_node(ev))
            if ev["kind"] == "spray":
                field = data["fields"][ev["field_id"]]
                obj += data["config"]["spray_setup_cost_per_field"]
                obj += data["config"]["late_penalty_per_priority_min"] * field["priority_weight"] * max(0.0, ev["start"] - field["preferred_latest_min"])
                obj += data["config"]["under_ideal_penalty_per_priority_liter"] * field["priority_weight"] * max(0.0, field["ideal_liters"] - ev["liters"])
            if ev["kind"] == "rendezvous":
                obj += data["config"]["wait_cost_per_min"] * max(0.0, ev["start"] - ev["arrival"])
            prev = event_node(ev)
    for tid, route in tender.items():
        prev = "DEPOT"
        for ev in route:
            obj += data["config"]["tender_travel_cost_per_km"] * dist_km(data, prev, event_node(ev))
            if ev["kind"] == "reload":
                obj += data["config"]["tender_reload_fixed_cost"]
            if ev["kind"] == "rendezvous":
                obj += data["config"]["wait_cost_per_min"] * max(0.0, ev["start"] - ev["arrival"])
            prev = event_node(ev)
    return len(errors) == 0, errors, {"objective_public_score": obj, "violated_groups": len(errors), "max_residual": 0.0 if not errors else 1.0}


def export_solution(sprayer, tender):
    out = {"sprayer_routes": [], "tender_routes": []}
    for sid, route in sprayer.items():
        events = []
        for ev in route:
            if ev["kind"] == "spray":
                events.append({
                    "event_id": ev["event_id"],
                    "event_type": "spray",
                    "field_id": ev["field_id"],
                    "arrival_min": round8(ev["arrival"]),
                    "start_min": round8(ev["start"]),
                    "end_min": round8(ev["end"]),
                    "applied_liters": round8(ev["liters"]),
                })
            elif ev["kind"] == "rinse":
                events.append({
                    "event_id": ev["event_id"],
                    "event_type": "rinse",
                    "pad_id": ev["pad_id"],
                    "arrival_min": round8(ev["arrival"]),
                    "start_min": round8(ev["start"]),
                    "end_min": round8(ev["end"]),
                    "from_mix_id": ev["from_mix_id"],
                    "to_mix_id": ev["to_mix_id"],
                })
            else:
                events.append({
                    "event_id": ev["event_id"],
                    "event_type": "rendezvous",
                    "sync_id": ev["sync_id"],
                    "pad_id": ev["pad_id"],
                    "tender_id": ev["tender_id"],
                    "arrival_min": round8(ev["arrival"]),
                    "start_min": round8(ev["start"]),
                    "end_min": round8(ev["end"]),
                    "mix_id": ev["mix_id"],
                    "refill_liters": round8(ev["liters"]),
                })
        out["sprayer_routes"].append({"sprayer_id": sid, "events": events})
    for tid, route in tender.items():
        events = []
        for ev in route:
            if ev["kind"] == "reload":
                events.append({
                    "event_id": ev["event_id"],
                    "event_type": "reload",
                    "arrival_min": round8(ev["arrival"]),
                    "start_min": round8(ev["start"]),
                    "end_min": round8(ev["end"]),
                    "mix_id": ev["mix_id"],
                    "loaded_liters": round8(ev["liters"]),
                })
            else:
                events.append({
                    "event_id": ev["event_id"],
                    "event_type": "rendezvous",
                    "sync_id": ev["sync_id"],
                    "sprayer_id": ev["sprayer_id"],
                    "pad_id": ev["pad_id"],
                    "arrival_min": round8(ev["arrival"]),
                    "start_min": round8(ev["start"]),
                    "end_min": round8(ev["end"]),
                    "mix_id": ev["mix_id"],
                    "delivered_liters": round8(ev["liters"]),
                })
        out["tender_routes"].append({"tender_id": tid, "events": events})
    return out


def main():
    tl = time_limit()
    data = load_data()
    liters = build_liters(data, tl)
    sprayer, tender = build_routes(data, liters)
    schedule(data, sprayer, tender, tl)
    ok, errors, metrics = validate(data, sprayer, tender)
    if not ok:
        raise RuntimeError("Validation failed:\n" + "\n".join(errors))
    solution = export_solution(sprayer, tender)
    with open(SOLUTION_PATH, "w") as f:
        json.dump(solution, f, indent=2)
    print(json.dumps(metrics, indent=2))

---
name: advanced-mip-patterns
description: Use when a task involves fixed charges, logical implications, min/max choices, sequencing, no-overlap, piecewise costs, routing-state logic, or other advanced MIP modeling patterns.
metadata:
  short-description: Advanced MIP formulation patterns
---

# Advanced MIP Patterns

## Use when

- The formulation needs logical implications, fixed charges, activation decisions, ordering, no-overlap, disjunctions, min/max, absolute values, tiers, route state, or resource calendars.
- A simple LP/MIP is producing unrealistic behavior because decisions are not linked tightly enough.
- The model is slow or infeasible and the likely cause is weak big-M logic, excessive pairwise variables, or missing state transitions.

## Do not use when

- A continuous LP is sufficient and adding binaries would not represent a real decision.
- The task is better handled by a specialized CP-SAT, constraint programming, nonlinear, or simulation method and the user did not require MIP.
- The user only needs a high-level explanation; do not introduce advanced constructs unless they clarify the actual model.

## General boundary rules

- Add binaries only for real yes/no choices, disjunctions, or nonconvex structure.
- Prefer the tightest formulation that matches the business rule; avoid decorative binaries.
- Use solver-native constructs when they improve clarity and are supported by the target environment.
- If a construct makes the model much slower, compare it with a simpler valid alternative before keeping it.

## Fixed charge and activation

- Use a binary `y` for whether an activity/resource is active.
- Link activity with a tight bound: `x <= U * y`.
- Add lower activation only when the business requires minimum use: `x >= L * y`.
- Charge fixed cost once through `fixed_cost * y`, not per unit.
- Do not force `y = 1` whenever `x = 0` unless the activity is mandatory.
- If there are startup/shutdown costs over time, separate active state from transition state.

## Logical rules

- Prefer solver-native indicator constraints for simple if-then rules when they keep the model clearer.
- If using big-M, derive M from data bounds and keep one M per rule family when possible.
- For either-or choices, use binaries whose sum is exactly one or at most one, matching the business rule.
- Distinguish implication (`A -> B`) from equivalence (`A <-> B`); the reverse direction often needs a separate constraint.
- For conditional capacity, link both activity and resource use to the condition.

## Min, max, absolute value

- For `z = max(a, b)`, add `z >= a`, `z >= b`, and objective pressure if minimizing z. Use binaries only when equality is needed without objective pressure.
- For absolute deviation `d = |x - target|`, add `d >= x - target` and `d >= target - x`.
- For service-level penalties, separate hard feasibility from soft penalty variables.
- For `min`, either transform with signs or add binaries when equality is not enforced by the objective.
- For ratio constraints, cross-multiply only when denominators are constant or safely bounded away from zero.

## Sequencing and no-overlap

- For jobs sharing a resource, use order binary `before[i,j]`.
- Typical pattern:
  - `start[j] >= end[i] - M * (1 - before[i,j])`
  - `start[i] >= end[j] - M * before[i,j]`
- Compute M from the scheduling horizon, not from an arbitrary large number.
- Include setup, cleanup, travel, rest, or turnover time in the correct side of the ordering constraint.
- Create pairwise order variables only for pairs that can share a resource or conflict.
- If time is discrete and the horizon is small, a time-indexed model may be cleaner than pairwise disjunctions.

## Piecewise and tiers

- Use solver-native piecewise-linear features for convex piecewise costs when suitable.
- For nonconvex tiers, use explicit segment variables and segment activation binaries.
- Ensure tier quantities sum to the original activity and each tier has its own upper bound.
- Distinguish incremental tiers from all-units tiers; their formulations and costs differ.
- Check whether discount tiers create nonconvex incentives that require binary selection.

## State and inventory logic

- State variables need initial conditions, transition equations, and terminal requirements if the business specifies them.
- Route or schedule state should be advanced in event order, not checked only row by row.
- Recovery, restock, cleaning, cooldown, maintenance, and rest rules often require cumulative state or last-event logic.
- If state can reset, model the reset trigger and its resource/time cost.

## Debugging advanced models

- First solve a relaxed or smaller instance and inspect whether the intended binaries activate.
- Add named constraints so IIS output points to business rules.
- If the reference solution is infeasible, replay the relevant state transitions manually before changing the evaluator.
- If the LP relaxation is very weak, inspect big-M values, missing lower/upper links, and symmetry.
- If the model is unexpectedly infeasible, test each advanced rule family on a tiny instance with a known feasible solution.

---
name: optimization-modeling-core
description: Use for natural-language-to-optimization tasks that require translating a business problem into a complete mathematical model before writing solver code.
metadata:
  short-description: Turn business text into a full optimization model
---

# Optimization Modeling Core

## Use when

- The user gives a word problem, business process, paper scenario, or benchmark prompt and expects a formal optimization model.
- Solver code exists but the issue appears to be a missing or incorrect formulation.
- The task asks for a complete model, not only a heuristic, simulation, or data summary.

## Do not use when

- The task is purely about software plumbing, visualization, data cleaning, or report writing.
- The problem is already fully formulated and the user only asks for code translation into a specific solver; use the solver-specific skill as the primary guide.
- The request is exploratory and the user explicitly wants brainstorming without implementation.

## Model-first workflow

1. Restate the business decision in plain language.
2. Identify sets, indices, units, and time granularity.
3. Extract parameters from data files and confirm units.
4. Define decision variables with domains and business meaning.
5. Write the objective with every cost, reward, penalty, and service term.
6. Write constraints grouped by operational rule.
7. Check edge cases: missing eligibility, impossible demand, fixed commitments, and terminal-state requirements.
8. Only then implement code.

## Ambiguity handling

- If the brief is ambiguous but data and examples imply a convention, state the assumption and continue.
- If ambiguity changes feasibility, objective direction, or deliverable schema, ask before coding or implement the least risky interpretation with a clear note.
- If units conflict across files, normalize units explicitly before modeling.
- If a hard requirement conflicts with data, diagnose the conflict before adding slack variables.

## Formulation checklist

- Every row in an input table should either become a parameter, an eligibility rule, or be explicitly irrelevant.
- Every decision variable should appear in at least one binding constraint and usually in the objective.
- Demand balance should use the correct equality or inequality direction; do not weaken equality to `>=` unless surplus is genuinely allowed.
- Capacity constraints should include activation/open/setup binaries when the business says inactive resources cannot produce, ship, or serve.
- Shortage, backlog, overtime, outsourcing, and postponement variables need explicit cost and bounds.
- Multi-period models need state transition equations and initial/terminal states.
- If the user asks for explanation, describe the modeling logic in business terms after solving.

## Constraint boundary rules

- Hard constraints represent physical impossibility, policy mandates, legal requirements, or explicit benchmark feasibility rules.
- Soft constraints need penalty variables or objective terms; do not silently turn hard constraints into penalties.
- Service levels should specify numerator, denominator, eligible population, and time window.
- Fairness constraints should define the comparison group and whether unavailable resources count.
- Logical implications need both directions only when the business rule says equivalence, not merely implication.

## Objective boundary rules

- Confirm whether the problem minimizes cost, maximizes profit, maximizes service, or uses lexicographic priorities.
- Do not mix penalties with primary economics unless their scale is intentional.
- Include fixed costs only once per activated entity.
- Include revenue only for fulfilled demand or accepted jobs, not for unmet or canceled work.
- Keep reporting metrics separate from optimized objective terms unless the prompt explicitly optimizes them.

## Implementation expectations

- Use structured parsing for CSV/JSON instead of manual string manipulation.
- Keep solver code, solution export, and validation code clearly separated.
- For benchmark or task environments, read the provided brief before coding and follow the expected output schema exactly.
- When comparing two answers, diagnose concrete formulation differences and objective consequences, not only feasibility.
- Write exported solutions at the precision expected by the checker; avoid unnecessary rounding.
- Preserve the input schema and output schema unless the user asks to redesign the benchmark.

## Verification boundary

- Verify feasibility independently from solver status when an evaluator or checker exists.
- Recompute objective from exported artifacts, not only from in-memory solver expressions.
- For benchmark tasks, create at least one negative check for each newly added rule when practical.
- For paper or real-world tasks, separate what is directly supported by the source from synthetic or assumed data.

## Red flags

- Objective terms that are counted twice or charged at the wrong marginal rate.
- Binary variables not linked tightly enough to continuous activity.
- Time periods shifted by one index.
- Average or fairness constraints that ignore the denominator or eligibility set.
- Public prompt files that reveal solver-ready variables or formulas when the benchmark is meant to test modeling translation.
- A feasible solution that exploits missing eligibility, free disposal, negative cycles, or unbounded production.
- A model that reports optimality after a time limit, infeasible status, or missing incumbent.

import csv

ROOT = Path("/app")
DATA_DIR = ROOT / "data"
SUBMISSIONS_DIR = ROOT / "submissions"

SCIP_GAP = 0.0005
MAX_RUN_SECONDS = 300.0
TOL = 1e-6


WINDOW_CHOICE = {
    ("SP01", "F01"): 0,
    ("SP01", "F25"): 0,
    ("SP01", "F02"): 0,
    ("SP01", "F03"): 0,
    ("SP01", "F05"): 0,
    ("SP01", "F04"): 0,
    ("SP01", "F06"): 1,
    ("SP01", "F07"): 1,

    ("SP04", "F35"): 0,
    ("SP04", "F36"): 0,
    ("SP04", "F22"): 0,
    ("SP04", "F23"): 0,
    ("SP04", "F24"): 0,
    ("SP02", "F09"): 0,
    ("SP02", "F28"): 0,
    ("SP02", "F10"): 0,
    ("SP02", "F11"): 0,
    ("SP02", "F12"): 0,
    ("SP02", "F29"): 1,
    ("SP02", "F30"): 1,
    ("SP02", "F13"): 1,
    ("SP02", "F14"): 0,
    ("SP02", "F15"): 0,
    ("SP02", "F16"): 0,
    ("SP03", "F17"): 0,
    ("SP03", "F18"): 0,
    ("SP03", "F31"): 0,
    ("SP03", "F32"): 0,
    ("SP03", "F19"): 0,
    ("SP03", "F20"): 0,
    ("SP03", "F21"): 0,
    ("SP03", "F33"): 0,
}

SYNC_TO_SPRAYER = {
    "SYNC_A1": "SP01",
    "SYNC_A2": "SP04",
    "SYNC_B1": "SP01",
    "SYNC_B2": "SP02",
    "SYNC_C1": "SP03",
    "SYNC_C2": "SP02",
}


def load_json(path):
    with open(path) as f:
        return json.load(f)


def load_csv(path):
    with open(path) as f:
        return list(csv.DictReader(f))


def load_data():
    data = {"config": load_json(DATA_DIR / "config.json")}
    data["fields"] = {}
    for row in load_csv(DATA_DIR / "fields.csv"):
        row = dict(row)
        for key in (
            "lat",
            "lon",
            "area_ha",
            "terrain_factor",
            "min_l_per_ha",
            "ideal_l_per_ha",
            "max_l_per_ha",
            "earliest_min",
            "preferred_latest_min",
            "latest_min",
            "priority_weight",
        ):
            row[key] = float(row[key])
        row["steep"] = int(row["steep"])
        row["min_liters"] = row["area_ha"] * row["min_l_per_ha"]
        row["ideal_liters"] = row["area_ha"] * row["ideal_l_per_ha"]
        row["max_liters"] = row["area_ha"] * row["max_l_per_ha"]
        data["fields"][row["field_id"]] = row
    data["sprayers"] = {}
    for row in load_csv(DATA_DIR / "sprayers.csv"):
        row = dict(row)
        for key in (
            "tank_capacity_liters",
            "initial_liters",
            "shift_start_min",
            "shift_end_min",
            "speed_kmph",
            "base_ha_per_hour",
            "nozzle_lpm",
        ):
            row[key] = float(row[key])
        row["steep_allowed"] = int(row["steep_allowed"])
        data["sprayers"][row["sprayer_id"]] = row
    data["tenders"] = {}
    for row in load_csv(DATA_DIR / "tenders.csv"):
        row = dict(row)
        for key in (
            "capacity_liters",
            "shift_start_min",
            "shift_end_min",
            "speed_kmph",
            "refill_rate_lpm",
            "depot_reload_rate_lpm",
        ):
            row[key] = float(row[key])
        data["tenders"][row["tender_id"]] = row
    data["mixes"] = {}
    for row in load_csv(DATA_DIR / "mixes.csv"):
        row = dict(row)
        row["rinse_min"] = float(row["rinse_min"])
        data["mixes"][row["mix_id"]] = row
    data["pads"] = {}
    for row in load_csv(DATA_DIR / "pads.csv"):
        row = dict(row)
        row["lat"] = float(row["lat"])
        row["lon"] = float(row["lon"])
        row["water_available"] = int(row["water_available"])
        row["supported_mixes"] = row["supported_mixes"].split("|")
        data["pads"][row["pad_id"]] = row
    data["weather"] = {}
    for row in load_csv(DATA_DIR / "weather_windows.csv"):
        key = (row["weather_zone"], row["mix_id"])
        data["weather"].setdefault(key, []).append((float(row["open_min"]), float(row["close_min"])))
    data["sensitive"] = [(r["field_a"], r["field_b"], float(r["min_start_gap_min"])) for r in load_csv(DATA_DIR / "sensitive_separations.csv")]
    data["clusters"] = [(r["cluster_id"], r["field_ids"].split("|"), float(r["max_elapsed_min"])) for r in load_csv(DATA_DIR / "cluster_windows.csv")]
    data["blackouts"] = {}
    for row in load_csv(DATA_DIR / "pad_blackouts.csv"):
        data["blackouts"].setdefault(row["pad_id"], []).append((float(row["start_min"]), float(row["end_min"])))
    data["pad_capacity"] = {r["pad_id"]: int(r["simultaneous_services"]) for r in load_csv(DATA_DIR / "pad_capacity.csv")}
    return data


def time_limit():
    raw = os.environ.get("ORCLAW_SOLVE_TIME_LIMIT_SECONDS")
    if raw is None:
        return MAX_RUN_SECONDS
    try:
        return min(float(raw), MAX_RUN_SECONDS)
    except ValueError:
        return MAX_RUN_SECONDS


def node_coords(data, node):
    if node == "DEPOT":
        return data["config"]["depot_lat"], data["config"]["depot_lon"]
    if node in data["fields"]:
        return data["fields"][node]["lat"], data["fields"][node]["lon"]
    return data["pads"][node]["lat"], data["pads"][node]["lon"]


def dist_km(data, a, b):
    lat1, lon1 = node_coords(data, a)
    lat2, lon2 = node_coords(data, b)
    p1 = math.radians(lat1)
    p2 = math.radians(lat2)
    dphi = math.radians(lat2 - lat1)
    dlambda = math.radians(lon2 - lon1)
    av = math.sin(dphi / 2.0) ** 2 + math.cos(p1) * math.cos(p2) * math.sin(dlambda / 2.0) ** 2
    return data["config"]["road_factor"] * 6371.0 * 2.0 * math.atan2(math.sqrt(av), math.sqrt(1.0 - av))


def sprayer_travel(data, sprayer_id, a, b):
    return 60.0 * dist_km(data, a, b) / data["sprayers"][sprayer_id]["speed_kmph"]


def tender_travel(data, tender_id, a, b):
    return 60.0 * dist_km(data, a, b) / data["tenders"][tender_id]["speed_kmph"]


def spray_service(data, sprayer_id, field_id, liters):
    sprayer = data["sprayers"][sprayer_id]
    field = data["fields"][field_id]
    area_term = 60.0 * field["area_ha"] * field["terrain_factor"] / sprayer["base_ha_per_hour"]
    nozzle_term = liters / sprayer["nozzle_lpm"]
    return data["config"]["spray_fixed_setup_min"] + max(area_term, nozzle_term)


def refill_service(data, tender_id, liters):
    return data["config"]["sync_setup_min"] + max(
        liters / data["config"]["sprayer_refill_rate_lpm"],
        liters / data["tenders"][tender_id]["refill_rate_lpm"],
    )


def reload_service(data, tender_id, liters):
    return liters / data["tenders"][tender_id]["depot_reload_rate_lpm"]


def rinse_service(data, from_mix, to_mix):
    if data["mixes"][from_mix]["residue_family"] == data["mixes"][to_mix]["residue_family"]:
        return 0.0
    return max(data["mixes"][from_mix]["rinse_min"], data["mixes"][to_mix]["rinse_min"])


def feasible_windows(data, sprayer_id, field_id, liters):
    field = data["fields"][field_id]
    dur = spray_service(data, sprayer_id, field_id, liters)
    out = []
    for open_min, close_min in data["weather"][(field["weather_zone"], field["mix_id"])]:
        lb = max(field["earliest_min"], open_min)
        ub = min(field["latest_min"], close_min) - dur
        if lb <= ub + TOL:
            out.append((lb, ub))
    return out


def round8(x):
    return round(float(x), 8)


def solve_prefix_liters(data, fields, target, tl):
    model = Model("liters")
    model.setParam("display/verblevel", 0)
    model.setParam("limits/time", tl)
    model.setParam("limits/gap", SCIP_GAP)
    q = {}
    obj = 0.0
    for fid in fields:
        field = data["fields"][fid]
        q[fid] = model.addVar(lb=field["min_liters"], ub=field["ideal_liters"], name=f"q[{fid}]")
        obj += data["config"]["under_ideal_penalty_per_priority_liter"] * field["priority_weight"] * (field["ideal_liters"] - q[fid])
    model.addCons(quicksum(q.values()) == target)
    model.setObjective(obj, "minimize")
    model.optimize()
    if model.getNSols() == 0:
        raise RuntimeError("No solution for prefix liters LP.")
    sol = model.getBestSol()
    return {fid: model.getSolVal(sol, q[fid]) for fid in fields}


def build_liters(data, tl):
    liters = {fid: field["ideal_liters"] for fid, field in data["fields"].items()}
    liters.update(solve_prefix_liters(data, ["F01", "F25", "F02", "F03"], data["sprayers"]["SP01"]["initial_liters"], tl))
    liters.update(solve_prefix_liters(data, ["F09", "F28", "F10", "F11"], data["sprayers"]["SP02"]["initial_liters"], tl))
    return liters

---
name: or-pyscipopt
description: Use when building, running, validating, or debugging Python optimization models that use PySCIPOpt and SCIP.
metadata:

# OR PySCIPOpt

## Use when

- The task asks for a Python optimization model using SCIP or `pyscipopt`.
- Existing solver code imports `pyscipopt`, sets SCIP parameters, writes `.lp`/`.mps`, or calls a SCIP-based checker.
- The user asks to run, debug, speed up, validate, or explain a SCIP-based solve.

## Do not use when

- The task only needs a mathematical formulation and no solver-specific code.
- The project clearly uses a different solver stack, such as Pyomo with CBC/HiGHS, OR-Tools CP-SAT, PuLP, CPLEX, or cvxpy, unless the user asks to port it to PySCIPOpt.
- The model is nonlinear or stochastic in a way that SCIP cannot handle directly; first decide whether it can be linearized or approximated.

## Environment rule

Use the Python environment specified by the user or the project. In this ORClaw workspace, the default solver environment is the `or` conda environment with PySCIPOpt available:

```bash
conda run -n or python <script.py>
```

If `pyscipopt` is missing, report the missing dependency, identify the command that failed, and try the project-specified environment before changing solver stacks.

## Workflow

1. Read the task brief and input files before coding.
2. Write the full mathematical model first: sets, parameters, decision variables, objective, and constraints.
3. Implement the model with explicit tuple keys and named constraints.
4. Solve with the selected Python command.
5. Export the solution using the exact schema required by the task.
6. Validate with the task checker or an independent recomputation of feasibility and objective.
7. If the solution is not accepted, diagnose whether the issue is formulation, data parsing, export formatting, tolerance, or evaluator mismatch before changing the objective or constraints.

## PySCIPOpt patterns

- Use `from pyscipopt import Model, quicksum`.
- Create sparse variable dictionaries explicitly, for example:

```python
x = {(i, j, t): model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"x[{i},{j},{t}]") for i, j, t in keys}
```

- Use `quicksum(...)` for large linear expressions.
- Use `model.addCons(expr <= rhs, name="capacity[k,t]")` for constraints.
- Use `model.setObjective(expr, "minimize")` or `model.setObjective(expr, "maximize")`.
- Set reproducibility and limits with SCIP parameters:

```python
model.setParam("limits/gap", 0.0005)
model.setParam("limits/time", time_limit)
model.setParam("display/verblevel", 0)
```

- After `model.optimize()`, inspect `model.getStatus()` and only read values if a solution exists.
- Read variable values with `model.getVal(x[key])`, not `x[key].X`.
- Read objective and bound with `model.getObjVal()` and `model.getDualbound()`.
- Read relative gap with `model.getGap()` when available.

## Modeling hygiene

- Calibrate every big-M from data. If a tight M is not obvious, derive a bound and document it in a short comment.
- Keep feasibility checks, objective recomputation, and reference-optimality comparison separate.
- Do not round exported decision values unless the schema explicitly requires it.
- For benchmark work, treat the evaluator or checker as the scoring authority and the reference solver as a validation baseline.
- Keep solver logs or a concise solve summary when the user needs auditability.
- Avoid changing data to make a model feasible unless the task is explicitly to design or repair an instance.

## Debugging boundaries

- If a model is infeasible, inspect hard lower bounds, conservation equations, and mutually exclusive binary logic before relaxing constraints.
- If objective values differ, recompute each objective component from exported decisions.
- If runtime is high, first remove dominated variables, tighten big-M values, add eligibility filters, and check whether binaries are really necessary.
- If SCIP returns an incumbent with a gap, say so clearly; do not call it exact optimality unless the status and gap support that claim.
- If an evaluator rejects a solution that the model considers feasible, replay the rejected rule directly from the exported file before changing solver code.

## Useful commands

```bash
python -c "import pyscipopt; print(pyscipopt.__version__)"
conda run -n or python -c "import pyscipopt; print(pyscipopt.__version__)"
<python-command> <solver-script.py>
<python-command> <checker-script.py> --solution <solution-file> --env-dir <task-dir>
```

def coords(node):
    if node=='DEPOT': return cfg['depot_lat'], cfg['depot_lon']
    if node in fields: return float(fields[node]['lat']), float(fields[node]['lon'])
    return float(pads[node]['lat']), float(pads[node]['lon'])

def dist(a,b):
    lat1,lon1=coords(a); lat2,lon2=coords(b)
    R=6371.0
    p1,p2=math.radians(lat1),math.radians(lat2)
    dphi=math.radians(lat2-lat1); dl=math.radians(lon2-lon1)
    x=math.sin(dphi/2)**2+math.cos(p1)*math.cos(p2)*math.sin(dl/2)**2
    return cfg['road_factor']*R*2*math.atan2(math.sqrt(x), math.sqrt(1-x))

def spr_travel(sp,a,b): return 60*dist(a,b)/float(sprayers[sp]['speed_kmph'])
def ten_travel(tn,a,b): return 60*dist(a,b)/float(tenders[tn]['speed_kmph'])
def field_service(sp,fid,liters):
    s=sprayers[sp]; f=fields[fid]
    area=float(f['area_ha']); terrain=float(f['terrain_factor'])
    area_term=60*area*terrain/float(s['base_ha_per_hour'])
    nozzle_term=float(liters)/float(s['nozzle_lpm'])
    return cfg['spray_fixed_setup_min']+max(area_term,nozzle_term)
def refill_service(tn,liters):
    return cfg['sync_setup_min']+max(float(liters)/cfg['sprayer_refill_rate_lpm'], float(liters)/float(tenders[tn]['refill_rate_lpm']))
def reload_service(tn,liters): return float(liters)/float(tenders[tn]['depot_reload_rate_lpm'])
def rinse_service(fm,tm):
    if mixes[fm]['residue_family']==mixes[tm]['residue_family']: return 0.0
    return max(float(mixes[fm]['rinse_min']), float(mixes[tm]['rinse_min']))

    f=fields[fid]; dur=field_service(sp,fid,liters)

    for a,b in weather[(f['weather_zone'],f['mix_id'])]:

        if lb<=ub+1e-6: out.append((lb,ub))

for route in sol['sprayer_routes']:
    sid=route['sprayer_id']; s=sprayers[sid]
    prev='DEPOT'; prev_end=float(s['shift_start_min']); mix=s['initial_mix_id']; tank=float(s['initial_liters'])
    for i,ev in enumerate(route['events']):
        node=ev.get('field_id') or ev.get('pad_id')
        arr=(float(s['shift_start_min']) if i==0 else prev_end)+spr_travel(sid, 'DEPOT' if i==0 else prev, node)
        start=float(ev['start_min']); end=float(ev['end_min']); arrival=float(ev['arrival_min'])
        if abs(arr-arrival)>1e-4: errors.append(f'{sid} arrival mismatch {ev[\"event_id\"]}: exp {arr} got {arrival}')
        if arrival-start>1e-4: errors.append(f'{sid} starts before arrival {ev[\"event_id\"]}')
        if end>float(s['shift_end_min'])+1e-6: errors.append(f'{sid} shift end violated {ev[\"event_id\"]}')
        if ev['event_type']=='spray':
            fid=ev['field_id']; liters=float(ev['applied_liters']); dur=field_service(sid,fid,liters)
            if abs((start+dur)-end)>1e-4: errors.append(f'{sid} duration mismatch {fid}: {dur} vs {end-start}')
            f=fields[fid]
            if fid in field_seen: errors.append(f'duplicate field {fid}')
            field_seen.add(fid)
            if mix"'!=f['"'mix_id']: errors.append(f'mix mismatch {sid} {fid} state {mix}')
            if int(f['steep']) and int(s['steep_allowed'])"'!=1: errors.append(f'"'steep violation {sid} {fid}')
            if tank+1e-6<liters: errors.append(f'tank shortage {sid} before {fid}: {tank}<{liters}')
            tank-=liters
            if liters<float(f['area_ha'])*float(f['min_l_per_ha'])-1e-6 or liters>float(f['area_ha'])*float(f['max_l_per_ha'])+1e-6:
                errors.append(f'liter bounds {fid}')
            wins=feasible_windows(sid,fid,liters)
            if not any(lb-1e-6<=start<=ub+1e-6 for lb,ub in wins): errors.append(f'window violation {sid} {fid} start {start} wins {wins}')
            spray_times[fid]=(start,end,sid)
        elif ev['event_type']=='rinse':
            pad=ev['pad_id']; dur=rinse_service(ev['from_mix_id'],ev['to_mix_id'])
            if abs((start+dur)-end)>1e-4: errors.append(f'rinse duration mismatch {sid} {ev[\"event_id\"]}')
            if int(pads[pad]['water_available'])"'!=1: errors.append(f'"'rinse no water {pad}')
            for a,b,r in blackouts.get(pad,[]):
                if not(end<=a+1e-6 or start>=b-1e-6): errors.append(f'blackout overlap {sid} {ev[\"event_id\"]} {pad} {a}-{b}')
            pad_intervals.append((pad,start,end,f'{sid}:{ev[\"event_id\"]}'))
            mix=ev['to_mix_id']; tank=0.0
        elif ev['event_type']=='rendezvous':
            pad=ev['pad_id']; liters=float(ev['refill_liters']); dur=refill_service(ev['tender_id'],liters)
            if abs((start+dur)-end)>1e-4: errors.append(f'refill duration mismatch {sid} {ev[\"event_id\"]}: {dur} vs {end-start}')
            if mix"'!=ev['"'mix_id']: errors.append(f'refill mix mismatch {sid} {ev[\"event_id\"]}')
            if tank+liters>float(s['tank_capacity_liters'])+1e-6: errors.append(f'overfill {sid} {ev[\"event_id\"]}')
            for a,b,r in blackouts.get(pad,[]):
                if not(end<=a+1e-6 or start>=b-1e-6): errors.append(f'blackout overlap {sid} {ev[\"event_id\"]} {pad} {a}-{b}')
            tank+=liters
            pad_intervals.append((pad,start,end,ev['sync_id']))
            sync_s[ev['sync_id']]=(sid,ev)
        prev=node; prev_end=end
if field_seen"'!=set(fields): errors.append(f'"'missing fields {sorted(set(fields)-field_seen)}')
for route in sol['tender_routes']:
    tid=route['tender_id']; t=tenders[tid]
    prev='DEPOT'; prev_end=float(t['shift_start_min']); onboard_mix=None; onboard=0.0
    for i,ev in enumerate(route['events']):
        node=ev.get('pad_id') or 'DEPOT'
        arr=float(t['shift_start_min']) if i==0 else prev_end+ten_travel(tid, prev, node)
        start=float(ev['start_min']); end=float(ev['end_min']); arrival=float(ev['arrival_min'])
        if abs(arr-arrival)>1e-4: errors.append(f'{tid} arrival mismatch {ev[\"event_id\"]}: exp {arr} got {arrival}')
        if arrival-start>1e-4: errors.append(f'{tid} starts before arrival {ev[\"event_id\"]}')
        if end>float(t['shift_end_min'])+1e-6: errors.append(f'{tid} shift end violated {ev[\"event_id\"]}')
        if ev['event_type']=='reload':
            liters=float(ev['loaded_liters']); dur=reload_service(tid,liters)
            if abs((start+dur)-end)>1e-4: errors.append(f'reload duration mismatch {tid} {ev[\"event_id\"]}: {dur} vs {end-start}')
            if liters>float(t['capacity_liters'])+1e-6: errors.append(f'reload cap exceeded {tid} {ev[\"event_id\"]}')
            onboard_mix=ev['mix_id']; onboard=liters
        else:
            liters=float(ev['delivered_liters']); dur=refill_service(tid,liters)
            if abs((start+dur)-end)>1e-4: errors.append(f'tender refill duration mismatch {tid} {ev[\"event_id\"]}')
            if onboard_mix"'!=ev['"'mix_id'] or onboard+1e-6<liters: errors.append(f'onboard mismatch {tid} {ev[\"event_id\"]}')
            sync_t[ev['sync_id']]=(tid,ev)
            onboard-=liters
        prev=node; prev_end=end
for sid,sev in sync_s.items():
    if sid not in sync_t:
        errors.append(f'missing tender sync {sid}')
        continue
    tid,tev=sync_t[sid]
    sev=sev[1]
    for k in [('pad_id','pad_id'),('mix_id','mix_id')]:
        if sev[k[0]]"'!=tev[k[1]]: errors.append(f'"'sync mismatch {sid} {k}')
    if abs(float(sev['start_min'])-float(tev['start_min']))>1e-4 or abs(float(sev['end_min'])-float(tev['end_min']))>1e-4:
        errors.append(f'sync timing mismatch {sid}')
    if abs(float(sev['refill_liters'])-float(tev['delivered_liters']))>1e-4:
        errors.append(f'sync liters mismatch {sid}')
for a,b,g in sensitive:
    if a in spray_times and b in spray_times:
        if abs(spray_times[a][0]-spray_times[b][0])+1e-6<g:
            errors.append(f'sensitive {a}-{b} gap {abs(spray_times[a][0]-spray_times[b][0])} < {g}')
for cid,fids,limit in clusters:
    first=min(spray_times[f][0] for f in fids)
    last=max(spray_times[f][1] for f in fids)
    if last-first>limit+1e-6:
        errors.append(f'cluster {cid} {last-first} > {limit}')
by_pad={}
for pad,s,e,label in pad_intervals:
    by_pad.setdefault(pad,[]).append((s,e,label))
for pad,seq in by_pad.items():
    seq.sort()
    for i in range(1,len(seq)):
        ps,pe,pl=seq[i-1]; s,e,l=seq[i]
        if s<pe-1e-6: errors.append(f'pad overlap {pad} {pl} with {l}')
        if s<pe+cfg['pad_turnover_min']-1e-6: errors.append(f'pad turnover {pad} {pl} to {l}')
obj=0.0
for route in sol['sprayer_routes']:
    sid=route['sprayer_id']; prev='DEPOT'
    for ev in route['events']:
        node=ev.get('field_id') or ev.get('pad_id')
        obj += cfg['sprayer_travel_cost_per_km']*dist(prev,node)
        if ev['event_type']=='spray':
            f=fields[ev['field_id']]
            obj += cfg['spray_setup_cost_per_field']
            obj += cfg['late_penalty_per_priority_min']*float(f['priority_weight'])*max(0.0, float(ev['start_min'])-float(f['preferred_latest_min']))
            obj += cfg['under_ideal_penalty_per_priority_liter']*float(f['priority_weight'])*max(0.0, float(f['area_ha'])*float(f['ideal_l_per_ha'])-float(ev['applied_liters']))
        if ev['event_type']=='rendezvous':
            obj += cfg['wait_cost_per_min']*max(0.0, float(ev['start_min'])-float(ev['arrival_min']))
        prev=node
for route in sol['tender_routes']:
    prev='DEPOT'
    for ev in route['events']:
        node=ev.get('pad_id') or 'DEPOT'
        obj += cfg['tender_travel_cost_per_km']*dist(prev,node)
        if ev['event_type']=='reload': obj += cfg['tender_reload_fixed_cost']
        if ev['event_type']=='rendezvous': obj += cfg['wait_cost_per_min']*max(0.0, float(ev['start_min'])-float(ev['arrival_min']))
        prev=node
print('VIOLATIONS',len(errors))
for e in errors[:100]: print(e)
print('OBJECTIVE',obj)

import json,csv,math
from pathlib import Path
from pyscipopt import Model, quicksum
root=Path('/app')
sol=json.load(open(root/'submissions/solution.json'))
cfg=json.load(open(root/'data/config.json'))
fields={r['field_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open(root/'data/fields.csv'))}
sprayers={r['sprayer_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open(root/'data/sprayers.csv'))}
tenders={r['tender_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open(root/'data/tenders.csv'))}
pads={r['pad_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open(root/'data/pads.csv'))}
mixes={r['mix_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open(root/'data/mixes.csv'))}
weather={}
for r in csv.DictReader(open(root/'data/weather_windows.csv')):
    weather.setdefault((r['weather_zone'],r['mix_id']), []).append((float(r['open_min']),float(r['close_min'])))
sensitive=[(r['field_a'],r['field_b'],float(r['min_start_gap_min'])) for r in csv.DictReader(open(root/'data/sensitive_separations.csv'))]
clusters=[(r['cluster_id'],r['field_ids'].split('|'),float(r['max_elapsed_min'])) for r in csv.DictReader(open(root/'data/cluster_windows.csv'))]
blackouts={}
for r in csv.DictReader(open(root/'data/pad_blackouts.csv')):
    blackouts.setdefault(r['pad_id'], []).append((float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min']),r['reason']))

def coords(node):
    if node=='DEPOT': return cfg['depot_lat'], cfg['depot_lon']
    if node in fields: return float(fields[node]['lat']), float(fields[node]['lon'])
    return float(pads[node]['lat']), float(pads[node]['lon'])

def dist(a,b):
    lat1,lon1=coords(a); lat2,lon2=coords(b)
    R=6371.0
    p1,p2=math.radians(lat1),math.radians(lat2)
    dphi=math.radians(lat2-lat1); dl=math.radians(lon2-lon1)
    x=math.sin(dphi/2)**2+math.cos(p1)*math.cos(p2)*math.sin(dl/2)**2
    return cfg['road_factor']*R*2*math.atan2(math.sqrt(x), math.sqrt(1-x))
def spr_travel(sp,a,b): return 60*dist(a,b)/float(sprayers[sp]['speed_kmph'])
def ten_travel(tn,a,b): return 60*dist(a,b)/float(tenders[tn]['speed_kmph'])
def field_service(sp,fid,liters):
    s=sprayers[sp]; f=fields[fid]
    area=float(f['area_ha']); terrain=float(f['terrain_factor'])
    return cfg['spray_fixed_setup_min']+max(60*area*terrain/float(s['base_ha_per_hour']), float(liters)/float(s['nozzle_lpm']))
def refill_service(tn,liters):
    return cfg['sync_setup_min']+max(float(liters)/cfg['sprayer_refill_rate_lpm'], float(liters)/float(tenders[tn]['refill_rate_lpm']))
def reload_service(tn,liters): return float(liters)/float(tenders[tn]['depot_reload_rate_lpm'])
def rinse_service(fm,tm):
    if mixes[fm]['residue_family']==mixes[tm]['residue_family']: return 0.0
    return max(float(mixes[fm]['rinse_min']), float(mixes[tm]['rinse_min']))
def feasible(sp,fid,liters):
    f=fields[fid]; d=field_service(sp,fid,liters); out=[]
    for a,b in weather[(f['weather_zone'],f['mix_id'])]:
        lb=max(float(f['earliest_min']),a); ub=min(float(f['latest_min']),b)-d
        if lb<=ub+1e-6: out.append((lb,ub))
    return out

def ev_node(ev): return ev.get('field_id') or ev.get('pad_id') or 'DEPOT'
def ev_duration(owner,ev):
    if ev['event_type']=='spray': return field_service(owner, ev['field_id'], ev['applied_liters'])
    if ev['event_type']=='rinse': return rinse_service(ev['from_mix_id'], ev['to_mix_id'])
    if ev['event_type']=='reload': return reload_service(owner, ev['loaded_liters'])
    if owner.startswith('TN'): return refill_service(owner, ev['delivered_liters'])
    return refill_service(ev['tender_id'], ev['refill_liters'])

model=Model('improve_timing'); model.setParam('display/verblevel',0); model.setParam('limits/time',60); model.setParam('limits/gap',0.0005)
start={}; late={}; wait={}

# Warm start from incumbent
warm = model.createSol()

# variables and route constraints

    sid=route['sprayer_id']; prev='DEPOT'; prev_end_expr=None; shift=float(sprayers[sid]['shift_start_min'])
    for i,ev in enumerate(route['events']):
        key=(sid,ev['event_id']); start[key]=model.addVar(lb=0.0,name=f'st[{sid},{ev[\"event_id\"]}]')
        model.setSolVal(warm, start[key], float(ev['start_min']))
        travel=spr_travel(sid, 'DEPOT' if i==0 else prev, ev_node(ev))
        arrival_expr=shift+travel if i==0 else start[prev_end_expr] + ev_duration(sid if prev in fields or prev=='DEPOT' or prev in pads else sid, prev_ev) + travel
        # above too awkward, replace below
        prev=ev_node(ev)

    blackouts.setdefault(r['pad_id'], []).append((float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min']),r['reason']))

def coords(node):
    if node=='DEPOT': return cfg['depot_lat'], cfg['depot_lon']
    if node in fields: return float(fields[node]['lat']), float(fields[node]['lon'])
    return float(pads[node]['lat']), float(pads[node]['lon'])

def dist(a,b):
    lat1,lon1=coords(a); lat2,lon2=coords(b)
    R=6371.0
    p1,p2=math.radians(lat1),math.radians(lat2)
    dphi=math.radians(lat2-lat1); dl=math.radians(lon2-lon1)
    x=math.sin(dphi/2)**2+math.cos(p1)*math.cos(p2)*math.sin(dl/2)**2
    return cfg['road_factor']*R*2*math.atan2(math.sqrt(x), math.sqrt(1-x))
def spr_travel(sp,a,b): return 60*dist(a,b)/float(sprayers[sp]['speed_kmph'])
def ten_travel(tn,a,b): return 60*dist(a,b)/float(tenders[tn]['speed_kmph'])
def field_service(sp,fid,liters):
    s=sprayers[sp]; f=fields[fid]
    area=float(f['area_ha']); terrain=float(f['terrain_factor'])
    return cfg['spray_fixed_setup_min']+max(60*area*terrain/float(s['base_ha_per_hour']), float(liters)/float(s['nozzle_lpm']))
def refill_service(tn,liters):
    return cfg['sync_setup_min']+max(float(liters)/cfg['sprayer_refill_rate_lpm'], float(liters)/float(tenders[tn]['refill_rate_lpm']))
def reload_service(tn,liters): return float(liters)/float(tenders[tn]['depot_reload_rate_lpm'])
def rinse_service(fm,tm):
    if mixes[fm]['residue_family']==mixes[tm]['residue_family']: return 0.0
    return max(float(mixes[fm]['rinse_min']), float(mixes[tm]['rinse_min']))
def feasible_windows(sp,fid,liters):
    f=fields[fid]; d=field_service(sp,fid,liters); out=[]
    for a,b in weather[(f['weather_zone'],f['mix_id'])]:
        lb=max(float(f['earliest_min']),a); ub=min(float(f['latest_min']),b)-d
        if lb<=ub+1e-6: out.append((lb,ub))
    return out

def ev_node(ev): return ev.get('field_id') or ev.get('pad_id') or 'DEPOT'
def ev_duration(owner, ev):
    if ev['event_type']=='spray': return field_service(owner, ev['field_id'], ev['applied_liters'])
    if ev['event_type']=='rinse': return rinse_service(ev['from_mix_id'], ev['to_mix_id'])
    if ev['event_type']=='reload': return reload_service(owner, ev['loaded_liters'])
    if owner.startswith('TN'): return refill_service(owner, ev['delivered_liters'])
    return refill_service(ev['tender_id'], ev['refill_liters'])

# choose current weather window for each spray
chosen_window={}
for route in sol['sprayer_routes']:
    sid=route['sprayer_id']
    for ev in route['events']:
        if ev['event_type']=='spray':
            st=float(ev['start_min'])
            wins=feasible_windows(sid, ev['field_id'], ev['applied_liters'])
            idx=None
            for i,(lb,ub) in enumerate(wins):
                if lb-1e-6<=st<=ub+1e-6:
                    idx=i; break
            chosen_window[(sid,ev['event_id'])]=idx

model=Model('timing_opt')
model.setParam('display/verblevel',0)
model.setParam('limits/time',60)
model.setParam('limits/gap',0.0005)
start={}; late={}; wait={}
warm=model.createSol()
all_events={}

for route in sol['sprayer_routes']:
    sid=route['sprayer_id']; shift=float(sprayers[sid]['shift_start_min'])
    prev_ev=None
    for i,ev in enumerate(route['events']):
        key=(sid,ev['event_id']); all_events[key]=(sid,ev)
        start[key]=model.addVar(lb=0.0,name=f'st[{sid},{ev[\"event_id\"]}]')
        model.setSolVal(warm, start[key], float(ev['start_min']))
        if i==0:
            arrival_expr=shift + spr_travel(sid, 'DEPOT', ev_node(ev))
        else:
            arrival_expr=start[(sid, prev_ev['event_id'])] + ev_duration(sid, prev_ev) + spr_travel(sid, ev_node(prev_ev), ev_node(ev))
        model.addCons(start[key] >= arrival_expr, name=f'seq_{sid}_{ev[\"event_id\"]}')
        if ev['event_type']=='spray':
            lb,ub=feasible_windows(sid, ev['field_id'], ev['applied_liters'])[chosen_window[key]]
            model.addCons(start[key] >= lb, name=f'winlb_{sid}_{ev[\"event_id\"]}')
            model.addCons(start[key] <= ub, name=f'winub_{sid}_{ev[\"event_id\"]}')
            late[key]=model.addVar(lb=0.0,name=f'late[{sid},{ev[\"event_id\"]}]')
            model.addCons(late[key] >= start[key] - float(fields[ev['field_id']]['preferred_latest_min']))
        elif ev['event_type'] in ('rinse','rendezvous'):
            for a,b,_ in blackouts.get(ev['pad_id'],[]):
                dur=ev_duration(sid,ev)
                if float(ev['end_min']) <= a + 1e-6:
                    model.addCons(start[key] + dur <= a, name=f'blk_before_{sid}_{ev[\"event_id\"]}_{int(a)}')
                elif float(ev['start_min']) >= b - 1e-6:
                    model.addCons(start[key] >= b, name=f'blk_after_{sid}_{ev[\"event_id\"]}_{int(b)}')
        model.addCons(start[key] + ev_duration(sid,ev) <= float(sprayers[sid]['shift_end_min']) + 1e-6)
        if ev['event_type']=='rendezvous':
            wait[key]=model.addVar(lb=0.0,name=f'wait[{sid},{ev[\"event_id\"]}]')
            model.addCons(wait[key] >= start[key] - arrival_expr)
        prev_ev=ev

for route in sol['tender_routes']:
    tid=route['tender_id']; shift=float(tenders[tid]['shift_start_min'])
    prev_ev=None
    for i,ev in enumerate(route['events']):
        key=(tid,ev['event_id']); all_events[key]=(tid,ev)
        start[key]=model.addVar(lb=0.0,name=f'st[{tid},{ev[\"event_id\"]}]')
        model.setSolVal(warm, start[key], float(ev['start_min']))
        if i==0:
            arrival_expr=shift
        else:
            arrival_expr=start[(tid, prev_ev['event_id'])] + ev_duration(tid, prev_ev) + ten_travel(tid, ev_node(prev_ev), ev_node(ev))
        model.addCons(start[key] >= arrival_expr, name=f'seq_{tid}_{ev[\"event_id\"]}')
        model.addCons(start[key] + ev_duration(tid,ev) <= float(tenders[tid]['shift_end_min']) + 1e-6)
        if ev['event_type']=='rendezvous':
            wait[key]=model.addVar(lb=0.0,name=f'wait[{tid},{ev[\"event_id\"]}]')
            model.addCons(wait[key] >= start[key] - arrival_expr)
        prev_ev=ev

# sync equalities
sync_s={}; sync_t={}
for route in sol['sprayer_routes']:
    sid=route['sprayer_id']
    for ev in route['events']:
        if ev['event_type']=='rendezvous': sync_s[ev['sync_id']] = (sid, ev['event_id'])
for route in sol['tender_routes']:
    tid=route['tender_id']
    for ev in route['events']:
        if ev['event_type']=='rendezvous': sync_t[ev['sync_id']] = (tid, ev['event_id'])
for sid in sync_s:
    model.addCons(start[sync_s[sid]] == start[sync_t[sid]], name=f'sync_{sid}')

# preserve incumbent order for sensitive pairs
field_to_key={}
for route in sol['sprayer_routes']:
    sid=route['sprayer_id']
    for ev in route['events']:
        if ev['event_type']=='spray': field_to_key[ev['field_id']] = (sid, ev['event_id'])
for a,b,g in sensitive:
    ka,kb=field_to_key[a], field_to_key[b]
    sa=next(ev for r in sol['sprayer_routes'] if r['sprayer_id']==ka[0] for ev in r['events'] if ev['event_id']==ka[1])
    sb=next(ev for r in sol['sprayer_routes'] if r['sprayer_id']==kb[0] for ev in r['events'] if ev['event_id']==kb[1])
    if float(sa['start_min']) <= float(sb['start_min']):
        model.addCons(start[kb] >= start[ka] + g, name=f'sens_{a}_{b}')
    else:
        model.addCons(start[ka] >= start[kb] + g, name=f'sens_{b}_{a}')

# cluster windows
for cid,fids,limit in clusters:
    alpha=model.addVar(lb=0.0,name=f'alpha[{cid}]')
    for fid in fids:
        k=field_to_key[fid]
        sid,eid=k
        ev=next(ev for r in sol['sprayer_routes'] if r['sprayer_id']==sid for ev in r['events'] if ev['event_id']==eid)
        model.addCons(alpha <= start[k])
        model.addCons(start[k] + ev_duration(sid,ev) <= alpha + limit)

# preserve incumbent order for pad uses and turnover
pad_events=[]
for route in sol['sprayer_routes']:
    sid=route['sprayer_id']
    for ev in route['events']:
        if ev['event_type'] in ('rinse','rendezvous'):
            pad_events.append((ev['pad_id'], float(ev['start_min']), (sid, ev['event_id']), ev_duration(sid,ev)))
pad_events.sort()
by_pad={}
for pad,st,key,d in pad_events: by_pad.setdefault(pad,[]).append((st,key,d))
for pad,seq in by_pad.items():
    seq.sort(key=lambda x:x[0])
    for i in range(1,len(seq)):
        _,kprev,dprev=seq[i-1]; _,kcur,_=seq[i]
        model.addCons(start[kcur] >= start[kprev] + dprev + cfg['pad_turnover_min'], name=f'pad_{pad}_{i}')

# objective: only variable terms under fixed structure/liters
obj=0
for route in sol['sprayer_routes']:
    sid=route['sprayer_id']
    for ev in route['events']:
        if ev['event_type']=='spray':
            field=fields[ev['field_id']]
            obj += cfg['late_penalty_per_priority_min']*float(field['priority_weight'])*late[(sid,ev['event_id'])]
        elif ev['event_type']=='rendezvous':
            obj += cfg['wait_cost_per_min']*wait[(sid,ev['event_id'])]
for route in sol['tender_routes']:
    tid=route['tender_id']
    for ev in route['events']:
        if ev['event_type']=='rendezvous':
            obj += cfg['wait_cost_per_min']*wait[(tid,ev['event_id'])]
obj += 1e-6*quicksum(start.values())
model.setObjective(obj,'minimize')
model.addSol(warm)
model.optimize()
print('status',model.getStatus(),'sols',model.getNSols(),'obj',model.getObjVal() if model.getNSols() else None)
if model.getNSols():
    s=model.getBestSol()
    for route in sol['sprayer_routes']:
        sid=route['sprayer_id']
        for ev in route['events']:
            print(sid, ev['event_id'], 'old', ev['start_min'], 'new', model.getSolVal(s,start[(sid,ev['event_id'])]))

        for ev in route['events']:
            print(tid, ev['event_id'], 'old', ev['start_min'], 'new', model.getSolVal(s,start[(tid,ev['event_id'])]))

tool33. Command (exit 0)
/bin/bash -lc "PYTHONPATH=/opt/or-deps python - <<'PY'
import json,csv,math
from pathlib import Path
root=Path('/app')
sol=json.load(open(root/'submissions/solution.json'))
cfg=json.load(open(root/'data/config.json'))
fields={r['field_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open(root/'data/fields.csv'))}
sprayers={r['sprayer_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open(root/'data/sprayers.csv'))}
tenders={r['tender_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open(root/'data/tenders.csv'))}
pads={r['pad_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open(root/'data/pads.csv'))}
mixes={r['mix_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open(root/'data/mixes.csv'))}
weather={}
for r in csv.DictReader(open(root/'data/weather_windows.csv')):
    weather.setdefault((r['weather_zone'],r['mix_id']), []).append((float(r['open_min']),float(r['close_min'])))
sensitive=[(r['field_a'],r['field_b'],float(r['min_start_gap_min'])) for r in csv.DictReader(open(root/'data/sensitive_separations.csv'))]
clusters=[(r['cluster_id'],r['field_ids'].split('|'),float(r['max_elapsed_min'])) for r in csv.DictReader(open(root/'data/cluster_windows.csv'))]
blackouts={}
for r in csv.DictReader(open(root/'data/pad_blackouts.csv')):
    blackouts.setdefault(r['pad_id'], []).append((float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min']),r['reason']))

def coords(node):
    if node=='DEPOT': return cfg['depot_lat'], cfg['depot_lon']
    if node in fields: return float(fields[node]['lat']), float(fields[node]['lon'])
    return float(pads[node]['lat']), float(pads[node]['lon'])

def dist(a,b):
    lat1,lon1=coords(a); lat2,lon2=coords(b)
    R=6371.0
    p1,p2=math.radians(lat1),math.radians(lat2)
    dphi=math.radians(lat2-lat1); dl=math.radians(lon2-lon1)
    x=math.sin(dphi/2)**2+math.cos(p1)*math.cos(p2)*math.sin(dl/2)**2
    return cfg['road_factor']*R*2*math.atan2(math.sqrt(x), math.sqrt(1-x))
def spr_travel(sp,a,b): return 60*dist(a,b)/float(sprayers[sp]['speed_kmph'])
def ten_travel(tn,a,b): return 60*dist(a,b)/float(tenders[tn]['speed_kmph'])
def field_service(sp,fid,liters):
    s=sprayers[sp]; f=fields[fid]
    area=float(f['area_ha']); terrain=float(f['terrain_factor'])
    return cfg['spray_fixed_setup_min']+max(60*area*terrain/float(s['base_ha_per_hour']), float(liters)/float(s['nozzle_lpm']))
def refill_service(tn,liters): return cfg['sync_setup_min']+max(float(liters)/cfg['sprayer_refill_rate_lpm'], float(liters)/float(tenders[tn]['refill_rate_lpm']))
def reload_service(tn,liters): return float(liters)/float(tenders[tn]['depot_reload_rate_lpm'])
def rinse_service(fm,tm):
    if mixes[fm]['residue_family']==mixes[tm]['residue_family']: return 0.0
    return max(float(mixes[fm]['rinse_min']), float(mixes[tm]['rinse_min']))
def feasible(sp,fid,liters):
    f=fields[fid]; d=field_service(sp,fid,liters); out=[]
    for a,b in weather[(f['weather_zone'],f['mix_id'])]:
        lb=max(float(f['earliest_min']),a); ub=min(float(f['latest_min']),b)-d
        if lb<=ub+1e-6: out.append((lb,ub))
    return out
errors=[]; field_seen=set(); spray_times={}; pad_intervals=[]; sync_s={}; sync_t={}
for route in sol['sprayer_routes']:
    sid=route['sprayer_id']; s=sprayers[sid]; prev='DEPOT'; prev_end=float(s['shift_start_min']); mix=s['initial_mix_id']; tank=float(s['initial_liters'])
    for i,ev in enumerate(route['events']):
        node=ev.get('field_id') or ev.get('pad_id'); arr=(float(s['shift_start_min']) if i==0 else prev_end)+spr_travel(sid, 'DEPOT' if i==0 else prev, node)
        start=float(ev['start_min']); end=float(ev['end_min']); arrival=float(ev['arrival_min'])
        if abs(arr-arrival)>1e-4: errors.append(f'{sid} arrival mismatch {ev[\"event_id\"]}')
        if arrival-start>1e-4: errors.append(f'{sid} starts before arrival {ev[\"event_id\"]}')
        if end>float(s['shift_end_min'])+1e-6: errors.append(f'{sid} shift end violated {ev[\"event_id\"]}')
        if ev['event_type']=='spray':
            fid=ev['field_id']; liters=float(ev['applied_liters'])
            if abs((start+field_service(sid,fid,liters))-end)>1e-4: errors.append(f'{sid} duration mismatch {fid}')
            f=fields[fid]
            if fid in field_seen: errors.append(f'duplicate field {fid}')
            field_seen.add(fid)
            if mix"'!=f['"'mix_id']: errors.append(f'mix mismatch {sid} {fid}')
            if int(f['steep']) and int(s['steep_allowed'])"'!=1: errors.append(f'"'steep violation {sid} {fid}')
            if tank+1e-6<liters: errors.append(f'tank shortage {sid} {fid}')
            tank-=liters
            if liters<float(f['area_ha'])*float(f['min_l_per_ha'])-1e-6 or liters>float(f['area_ha'])*float(f['max_l_per_ha'])+1e-6: errors.append(f'liter bounds {fid}')
            if not any(lb-1e-6<=start<=ub+1e-6 for lb,ub in feasible(sid,fid,liters)): errors.append(f'window {sid} {fid}')
            spray_times[fid]=(start,end)
        elif ev['event_type']=='rinse':
            if abs((start+rinse_service(ev['from_mix_id'],ev['to_mix_id']))-end)>1e-4: errors.append(f'rinse duration {sid} {ev[\"event_id\"]}')
            for a,b,r in blackouts.get(ev['pad_id'],[]):
                if not (end<=a+1e-6 or start>=b-1e-6): errors.append(f'blackout {sid} {ev[\"event_id\"]}')
            pad_intervals.append((ev['pad_id'],start,end,ev['event_id']))
            mix=ev['to_mix_id']; tank=0
        else:
            liters=float(ev['refill_liters'])
            if abs((start+refill_service(ev['tender_id'],liters))-end)>1e-4: errors.append(f'refill duration {sid} {ev[\"event_id\"]}')
            for a,b,r in blackouts.get(ev['pad_id'],[]):
                if not (end<=a+1e-6 or start>=b-1e-6): errors.append(f'blackout {sid} {ev[\"event_id\"]}')
            if tank+liters>float(s['tank_capacity_liters'])+1e-6: errors.append(f'overfill {sid} {ev[\"event_id\"]}')
            tank+=liters; pad_intervals.append((ev['pad_id'],start,end,ev['sync_id'])); sync_s[ev['sync_id']]=ev
        prev=node; prev_end=end
for route in sol['tender_routes']:
    tid=route['tender_id']; t=tenders[tid]; prev='DEPOT'; prev_end=float(t['shift_start_min']); onboard_mix=None; onboard=0
    for i,ev in enumerate(route['events']):
        node=ev.get('pad_id') or 'DEPOT'; arr=float(t['shift_start_min']) if i==0 else prev_end+ten_travel(tid, prev, node)
        start=float(ev['start_min']); end=float(ev['end_min']); arrival=float(ev['arrival_min'])
        if abs(arr-arrival)>1e-4: errors.append(f'{tid} arrival mismatch {ev[\"event_id\"]}')
        if arrival-start>1e-4: errors.append(f'{tid} starts before arrival {ev[\"event_id\"]}')
        if end>float(t['shift_end_min'])+1e-6: errors.append(f'{tid} shift end {ev[\"event_id\"]}')
        if ev['event_type']=='reload':
            liters=float(ev['loaded_liters'])
            if abs((start+reload_service(tid,liters))-end)>1e-4: errors.append(f'reload duration {tid} {ev[\"event_id\"]}')
            if liters>float(t['capacity_liters'])+1e-6: errors.append(f'cap {tid} {ev[\"event_id\"]}')
            onboard_mix=ev['mix_id']; onboard=liters
        else:
            liters=float(ev['delivered_liters'])
            if abs((start+refill_service(tid,liters))-end)>1e-4: errors.append(f'tender refill duration {tid} {ev[\"event_id\"]}')
            if onboard_mix"'!=ev['"'mix_id'] or onboard+1e-6<liters: errors.append(f'inventory {tid} {ev[\"event_id\"]}')
            onboard-=liters; sync_t[ev['sync_id']]=ev
        prev=node; prev_end=end
for sid,sev in sync_s.items():
    tev=sync_t.get(sid)
    if tev is None: errors.append(f'missing sync {sid}')
    elif abs(float(sev['start_min'])-float(tev['start_min']))>1e-4 or abs(float(sev['end_min'])-float(tev['end_min']))>1e-4 or abs(float(sev['refill_liters'])-float(tev['delivered_liters']))>1e-4 or sev['pad_id']"'!=tev['"'pad_id'] or sev['mix_id']"'!=tev['"'mix_id']:
        errors.append(f'sync mismatch {sid}')
for a,b,g in sensitive:
    if abs(spray_times[a][0]-spray_times[b][0])+1e-6<g: errors.append(f'sensitive {a}-{b}')
for cid,fids,limit in clusters:
    if max(spray_times[f][1] for f in fids)-min(spray_times[f][0] for f in fids)>limit+1e-6: errors.append(f'cluster {cid}')
by_pad={}
for pad,s,e,label in pad_intervals: by_pad.setdefault(pad,[]).append((s,e,label))
for pad,seq in by_pad.items():
    seq.sort()
    for i in range(1,len(seq)):
        if seq[i][0]<seq[i-1][1]-1e-6: errors.append(f'pad overlap {pad}')
        if seq[i][0]<seq[i-1][1]+cfg['pad_turnover_min']-1e-6: errors.append(f'pad turnover {pad}')
obj=0
for route in sol['sprayer_routes']:
    prev='DEPOT'
    for ev in route['events']:
        node=ev.get('field_id') or ev.get('pad_id')
        obj += cfg['sprayer_travel_cost_per_km']*dist(prev,node)
        if ev['event_type']=='spray':
            f=fields[ev['field_id']]
            obj += cfg['spray_setup_cost_per_field']
            obj += cfg['late_penalty_per_priority_min']*float(f['priority_weight'])*max(0.0,float(ev['start_min'])-float(f['preferred_latest_min']))
            obj += cfg['under_ideal_penalty_per_priority_liter']*float(f['priority_weight'])*max(0.0,float(f['area_ha'])*float(f['ideal_l_per_ha'])-float(ev['applied_liters']))
        if ev['event_type']=='rendezvous': obj += cfg['wait_cost_per_min']*max(0.0,float(ev['start_min'])-float(ev['arrival_min']))
        prev=node
for route in sol['tender_routes']:
    prev='DEPOT'
    for ev in route['events']:
        node=ev.get('pad_id') or 'DEPOT'
        obj += cfg['tender_travel_cost_per_km']*dist(prev,node)
        if ev['event_type']=='reload': obj += cfg['tender_reload_fixed_cost']
        if ev['event_type']=='rendezvous': obj += cfg['wait_cost_per_min']*max(0.0,float(ev['start_min'])-float(ev['arrival_min']))
        prev=node
print(json.dumps({'violations':len(errors),'objective':obj,'sample_errors':errors[:10]}, indent=2))
